摘要翻译#

中文标题: Wasserstein 梯度流形式下的浸入边界电扩散：全局适定性、非平衡热力学与奇异渐近

摘要翻译: 我们为浸入边界（IB）电扩散主动泵模型引入一个统一的最优传输框架。通过用光滑化的 Euler 核（$Ψ_w, \mathcal{H}_w$）表示膜化学势垒和主动泵势，我们将被动动力学表述为与表示 ATP 驱动的 $3:2$ $\text{Na}^+/\text{K}^+$ 交换体的非保守主动推挤场耦合的 $L^2$-Wasserstein（$W_2$）梯度流。我们通过分裂步 Jordan–Kinderlehrer–Otto（JKO）变分格式，建立连续 Poisson–Nernst–Planck–IB 系统解的全局弱存在性，其依据是非局部 Poisson 漂移的 Calderón–Zygmund 界以及 Stokes 流下 $H^3(\mathbb{S}^1)$ 界面正则性。我们证明连续 Rayleighian 能量平衡，并在定量 Bakry–Émery 曲率阈值下建立非平衡稳态（NESS）的指数 $W_2$-稳定性。此外，我们分析核平滑宽度 $w \to 0$ 与无量纲 Debye 长度 $g = λ_D / L \to 0$ 的对偶奇异极限。我们建立连接正则化模型与尖锐模型的精确渐近区域，证明正则化能量泛函的 $Γ$-收敛，推导分布意义下的界面通量跳跃条件，并求解 $O(λ_D)$ Debye 空间电荷边界层以确认体电中性。

不相关论文（基本信息）#

1. 自适应网格上积分分数阶拉普拉斯算子的鲁棒多重网格#

Robust Multigrid on Adaptive Meshes for the Integral Fractional Laplacian

作者: Markus Faustmann, Paula Hilbert, Ani Miraçi, Dirk Praetorius

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-04

arXiv ID: 2611.06577v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06577v1

摘要: We analyze a geometric multigrid method on locally refined mesh hierarchies for non-local variational problems, focusing on the integral fractional Laplacian. For newest-vertex-bisection-generated hierarchies, we prove uniform contraction of the multigrid iteration independently of the refinement level, the smallest local mesh size, and the polynomial degree in the finite element discretization. The proof is based on an abstract Hilbert-space framework, a strengthened Cauchy–Schwarz estimate obtained from a near-/far-field decomposition of the non-local bilinear form, and a multilevel stable decomposition independent of the discretization parameters. Numerical experiments underline the theoretical findings.

2. 计算力学中边值问题的一种张量网络 Chebyshev–Galerkin 方法#

A Tensor-Network Chebyshev–Galerkin Method for Boundary-Value Problems in Computational Mechanics

作者: Juan José Rodríguez-Aldavero, Ángel de la Rosa Velasco, Juan José García-Ripoll

类别: math.NA, quant-ph

发布日期: 2026-11-04

arXiv ID: 2611.06552v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06552v1

摘要: Quantum-inspired numerical methods for partial differential equations represent discretized fields and operators as quantics tensor trains (QTT), so that their cost depends on how compressible the problem is rather than on the number of unknowns. Global spectral formulations in this setting have so far relied on Fourier bases, restricted to periodic problems, or on collocation, which does not preserve the variational structure exploited by tensor-network solvers. We introduce a QTT Chebyshev–Galerkin method that assembles and solves global spectral systems directly in compressed form, without forming the underlying arrays. Starting from the weak formulation, the method handles coupled elliptic systems with variable coefficients, smoothly mapped geometries, and mixed Dirichlet–Robin boundary conditions, and yields symmetric positive-definite systems suited to variational tensor-network solvers. Parameters and random inputs enter as additional coordinates, so the same construction returns the solution over an entire parameter space in a single solve. In linear elasticity, the method retains spectral convergence and reaches errors below $10^{-8}$ on a three-dimensional benchmark, beyond those attained by QTT finite differences and Q2/Q4 FEniCSx finite elements over the tested range. A six-parameter cantilever design study covering $1.7\times10^{7}$ designs is solved in a single 92~s run, and a stochastic problem with ten random coordinates is represented with $2.4\times10^{6}$ times less memory than its full coefficient array.

3. 最大角二分与三角形形状的统计正则化#

Largest-Angle Bisection and Statistical Regularization of Triangle Shapes

作者: Jérôme Michaud

类别: math.DS, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-04

arXiv ID: 2611.06492v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06492v1

摘要: Largest-angle bisection (LAB) repeatedly divides a triangle by bisecting its largest angle. Although this procedure is known to preserve nondegenerate triangle shapes, less is known about the distribution of shapes across the resulting refinement tree. We show that its long-term behavior is governed by a distinction between pure and mixed branches. A branch that eventually selects only one of the two children may approach an exceptional family of shapes. By contrast, every branch that changes children infinitely often converges in shape to a right-isosceles triangle. This follows from a projective Lyapunov function that is non-increasing at every refinement and decreases strictly whenever the selected child changes. Consequently, a random branch converges to the right-isosceles shape with probability one, and the proportion of triangles near that shape tends to one across complete refinement levels. Largest-angle bisection therefore produces statistical regularization even though exceptional branches remain present.

4. 用于非线性自由表面波的基于残差 Dirichlet–Neumann 通量的保体积 ALE 连续 Galerkin 方法#

A volume-conserving ALE continuous Galerkin method with residual-based Dirichlet–Neumann fluxes for nonlinear free-surface waves

作者: Fan Wang, Yinhua Xia, Yan Xu

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-04

arXiv ID: 2611.06408v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06408v1

摘要: We develop an arbitrary Lagrangian–Eulerian continuous Galerkin finite element method for two-dimensional fully nonlinear free-surface potential flow. An isoparametric finite element method computes the interior potential on the current fluid domain, while a residual-based Dirichlet–Neumann flux drives the surface evolution. The residual-based flux satisfies a global discrete balance inherited from the interior variational formulation. When combined with compatible mesh motion, surface projection, and quadrature, this balance yields conservation of the discrete fluid volume at the semi-discrete level. An optional volume-conserving sixth-order surface dissipation controls high-frequency oscillations, and the surface equations are advanced with fourth-order Runge–Kutta integration, updating the mesh and interior solution at every stage. Numerical experiments confirm the expected spatial convergence for linear free-surface waves and demonstrate reduced mesh sensitivity for nonlinear waves over flat and curved bottoms. A rotating-wavemaker example further illustrates the treatment of moving solid boundaries.

5. 耦合 Stokess-Biot 问题的弱 Galerkin 有限元方法#

A weak Galerkin finite element method for the coupled Stokess-Biot problem

作者: Rui Li, Hui Peng, Xiuli Wang

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-04

arXiv ID: 2611.06335v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06335v1

摘要: In this paper,we consider a stable numerical method for the Stokes-Biot model. We adopt the standard formulation of the Stokes equations coupled with the mixed formulation of the Biot system involving the additional total pressure variable. We construct the semi-discrete scheme on polygonal meshes by employing weak Galerkin finite element methods for spatial discretization. It is convenient to incorporate the interface conditions into the semi-discrete scheme via the edge-based degrees of freedom. Furthermore, we give a fully discrete scheme based on the backward Euler method. We establish the well-posedness of these two schemes and provide a rigorous error analysis. Finally, we present some numerical examples to verify the convergence and stability of our algorithm under a wide range of some physical parameters.

6. 各向异性相场枝晶生长模型的线性自适应变时间步长格式#

Linear adaptive variable time step schemes for the anisotropic phase field dendritic crystal growth model

作者: Yayu Guo, Ziqi Ma

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-04

arXiv ID: 2611.05977v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05977v1

摘要: In this paper, we propose a class of efficient, linear and decoupled adaptive variable time step schemes for the anisotropic phase field dendritic crystal growth model. The main difficulty is twofold: the gradient dependent anisotropic coefficient and the strongly nonlinear terms make unconditional energy stability difficult to achieve, and on nonuniform meshes the discrete energy dissipation law, which is straightforward to establish on uniform meshes, has to be reestablished under varying step sizes. To overcome these difficulties, two linear stabilizers and a time dependent auxiliary variable with relaxation are introduced and incorporated into a second-order generalized BDF-type operator, with the spatial derivatives discretized by the Fourier spectral method. The proposed schemes are energy stable for arbitrary positive adjacent step ratios, so that no restriction on the step ratio is imposed, and only two linear equations together with a few algebraic equations, which are solved independently in Fourier space, need to be solved at each time step. Moreover, the schemes are naturally compatible with energy based time adaptive strategies. The associated adaptive strategy reduces the number of time steps by $24.6\%$–$86.6\%$ in comparison with uniform time stepping, and numerical experiments on fourfold and sixfold dendritic growth in two dimensions and on three-dimensional growth confirm the accuracy, efficiency and stability of the proposed schemes.

7. 非均匀周期磁场中的磁 Bloch 特征值问题：一种 Fourier 有限差分方法#

Magnetic Bloch eigenvalue problems in nonuniform periodic magnetic fields: a Fourier-finite difference approach

作者: Xueyang Li, Yuanwei Lai

类别: math.NA, quant-ph

发布日期: 2026-11-04

arXiv ID: 2611.05950v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05950v1

摘要: We study magnetic Bloch eigenvalue problems for two-dimensional magnetic Schrödinger operators with nonuniform periodic magnetic fields and periodic scalar potentials, and develop a hybrid Fourier-finite difference discretization. A Landau-type gauge is constructed by solving a periodic Poisson equation. Under the integer magnetic flux condition, the magnetic Bloch boundary conditions are incorporated through shifts of Fourier-mode indices. The method combines link-variable-based covariant central differences in the $x$-direction with a Fourier pseudospectral discretization in the $y$-direction, yielding a Hermitian discrete Hamiltonian. The resulting eigenvalue problems are solved using a LOBPCG method. Under appropriate regularity assumptions, we establish a fully discrete consistency residual estimate of order $O(h_1^2+N_2^{-s})$. With suitable spectral separation and eigenspace dimension-preserving conditions, error estimates of the same order are derived for isolated eigenvalues and their associated eigenspaces. Numerical experiments show second-order convergence in the finite difference direction and rapid error reduction with increasing Fourier resolution, and illustrate band degeneracies and eigenstate distributions in nonuniform periodic magnetic fields.

8. 维数绝对稳定但不可对角化的 $T$-网格#

Dimensionally absolutely stable but non-diagonalizable $T$-meshes

作者: Mao Shi

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-04

arXiv ID: 2611.05923v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05923v1

摘要: We construct an infinite family of rectangular (T)-meshes whose conformality-vector-space dimension is constant over all structurally similar geometric realizations, although the meshes are not diagonalizable. For every integer (d\ge 2), the construction has (2(d+2)) (\Tl)-edges and intersection graph (K_{d+2,d+2}). Every (\Tl)-edge contains at least (d+2) vertices, so no edge is vanishable under the usual vertex-count criterion for bidegree ((d,d)) and smoothness ((d-1,d-1)). We prove that the conformality-vector space is one-dimensional throughout the structural similarity class. Its generator is the outer product of two vectors of barycentric interpolation weights, extended by four zero coordinates. Non-diagonalizability follows by examining the last edge in any proposed diagonalizing ordering. Thus, for the geometric structural similarity class specified here, diagonalizability is not a necessary condition for dimensional absolute stability.

9. 稀疏视角下X射线三维重建数值方法的比较分析#

Comparative analysis of numerical methods for X-ray 3D reconstruction from sparse views

作者: Melitine N. Bardi, Konstantinos K. Delibasis

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.05645v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05645v1

摘要: Reconstruction of three-dimensional vascular structures from a limited number of X-ray projections is a severely underdetermined and ill-conditioned inverse problem. This work presents a unified matrix-free computational framework for evaluating iterative numerical methods for sparse-view cone-beam tomography. Forward and backprojection are implemented from ray-voxel intersection lengths computed with the Amanatides-Woo traversal algorithm, avoiding explicit construction of the large sparse system matrix. We systematically compare ART, SART, SIRT, gradient descent (GD), and Krylov subspace methods, including Conjugate Gradient, LSQR, and GMRES. The common matrix-free operators and geometry are used for all methods, allowing consistent comparisons under matched conditions. Particular emphasis is placed on gradient scaling using scalar step sizes, Jacobi preconditioning, and diagonal Hessian majorization, together with L1, Tikhonov, and total variation regularization. Numerical experiments use five three-dimensional coronary vessel models reconstructed from ten simulated X-ray projections under noise and scattering conditions, and are extended to real X-ray projections of a static thin-vessel phantom. Performance is evaluated in terms of convergence, computational cost, volumetric overlap, contrast-to-noise ratio, vessel diameter and centerline errors, and projection consistency at an unseen viewing angle. The results show that total-variation-regularized gradient descent with diagonal majorization provides the best overall reconstruction performance within the investigated computational budgets. By contrast, the tested Krylov implementations achieve limited geometric recovery despite higher computational cost. These findings emphasize the importance of appropriate gradient scaling and regularization for stable and computationally efficient sparse-view tomographic reconstruction.

10. 优化 Schwarz 波形松弛中作为迭代次数、重叠量和时间窗口长度函数的首个闭式 Robin 参数#

A First Closed-Form Robin Parameter for Optimized Schwarz Waveform Relaxation as a Function of Iteration Count, Overlap, and Time Window Length

作者: Alejandro Alfonso Rodriguez, Martin J. Gander, Felix Kwok

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.05576v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05576v1

摘要: Schwarz waveform relaxation (SWR) is a parallel-in-time iterative method for the solution of evolution problems and belongs to the class of Parallel-in-Time algorithms that have received increasing attention over the past three decades. For parabolic problems, SWR converges linearly over long time intervals and exhibits superlinear convergence on short time intervals. Since the introduction of optimized variants (OSWR) nearly three decades ago, determining the optimized Robin parameter when OSWR is applied on bounded time windows has remained an open problem. We introduce here a new analytical approach that, for the first time, yields a closed-form expression for the optimal Robin parameter in the transmission conditions for the one-dimensional heat equation with two overlapping subdomains. The resulting formula explicitly characterizes the dependence of the optimal parameter on the iteration number, the overlap size, and the length of the time window. Beyond resolving this long-standing optimization problem, our analysis provides a framework that can be extended to more general settings, including higher-dimensional problems, multiple subdomains, and higher-order transmission conditions.

11. 单精度时间积分中更宽的状态，而非更宽的算术#

A wider state, not wider arithmetic, for single-precision time integration

作者: Michael F. Barad

类别: math.NA, physics.flu-dyn

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.05365v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05365v1

摘要: Single precision (FP32) halves the memory and data movement of double precision (FP64), and on accelerators is often several times faster. Its rounding errors are harmless except in the stored state. A modified equation for Runge-Kutta methods in Shu-Osher form and linear multistep methods separates the rounding of the stored state, a forcing proportional to $1/Δt$, from truncation and from right-hand-side rounding, which sets a $Δt$-independent floor. We bound this forcing for strong-stability-preserving schemes and identify two mechanisms that keep its sign fixed, so it accumulates as a bias: past a turnaround step, refining the step increases the error, in FP64 as in FP32. For a model oscillator the analysis predicts the floor and the FP32-to-FP64 ratios of turnaround step and minimum error within 30%. Our remedy needs no FP64 arithmetic: each state variable is stored as the unevaluated sum of two FP32 numbers, updated with error-free transformations, while every right-hand-side evaluation stays in FP32. Unlike compensated summation, it needs no rewriting of the scheme. In model problems from geophysical flow, mechanical dynamics and celestial mechanics, with five Runge-Kutta and multistep schemes, it restores design-order convergence to the floor. In large-eddy simulations of a high-lift airplane wing on three refined grids, up to 544 million cells, with time steps $Δt$, $Δt/2$ and $Δt/4$, FP32 overpredicts the lift by 2.1, 4.5 and 7.7%, while the two-float lift agrees with FP64 within 0.3% on every grid, and on graphics processing units the production solver runs 1.6 to 2.7 times faster than FP64.

12. 基于直接插值与约束优化的 ALE 流体力学重映射#

Remap for ALE Hydrodynamics via Direct Interpolation and Constraint Optimization

作者: Brendan Keith, Dohyun Kim, Boyan Lazarov, Ketan Mittal, Cosmin G. Petra, Mathias R. Schmidt, Vladimir Z. Tomov

类别: math.NA, physics.comp-ph

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.05327v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05327v1

摘要: This work presents an optimization-based remap approach for arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) hydrodynamics that preserves sharp features and physical bounds while conserving global quantities such as volume, mass, momentum, and total energy. Achieving these properties is the main challenge in remap methods. The presented approach is well-suited for high-order finite element (FE) discretizations and ALE methods that allow for large, purely Lagrangian displacements between successive remeshing and remapping steps. Recent advances in parallel, GPU-capable interpolation routines enable the initial transfer of all fields via direct physical-space interpolation, avoiding computationally costly and diffusive pseudo-transient PDE-based advection. The interpolation produces bounded fields with minimal numerical diffusion. However, it may violate the desired conservation of global properties. Thus, a subsequent optimization step restores conservation while preserving physical bounds. The presented method provides minimally diffusive remapping of finite element fields of arbitrary order. Moreover, it directly remaps quantities defined at integration points, eliminating the need for projection onto finite element spaces. The approach also mitigates typical challenges associated with synchronizing primal variables and conserved quantities in advection-based remap methods. The proposed optimization-based remap method is evaluated using standard two- and three-dimensional remap benchmarks and full ALE hydrodynamics simulations.

13. 用于 P1 和 Q1 单元的带局部非奇异性认证的统一等参移动网格 PDE 方法#

A Unified Isoparametric Moving Mesh PDE Method for P1 and Q1 Elements with Certified Local Nonsingularity

作者: Xianping Li

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.05026v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05026v1

摘要: We develop a unified isoparametric moving mesh partial differential equation (MMPDE) method for $P_1$ simplicial and $Q_1$ tensor-product elements, including elements on embedded surfaces. Mesh movement follows a prescribed symmetric positive-definite metric through equidistribution and alignment. For $Q_1$ elements, we prove that bounded, exactly integrated adaptation energy prevents local Jacobian rank loss on a fixed finite mesh at sharp critical exponents. The result also gives local full-rank preservation for embedded bilinear surface elements. A complete physical-coordinate gradient accounts for metric variation at moving quadrature points. For the discrete MMPDE, we use whole-element validity tests and develop sufficient Bernstein certificates for trilinear hexahedra. An explicit positive-margin bound on each fixed stage is provided by an optional certificate barrier. Numerical examples illustrate metric-driven movement on one-dimensional, planar, volume, and graph-surface meshes. Limitations of pointwise hexahedral Jacobian sampling are also presented.

14. 带记忆的分数阶热方程的障碍问题#

The obstacle problem for fractional heat equations with memory

作者: Félix del Teso, Fernando Quirós, Julio D. Rossi

类别: math.AP, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.04971v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04971v1

摘要: We study the obstacle problem for the evolution equation $\partial_t^αu+(-Δ)^s u=0$ in the whole space $\mathbb{R}^d$, where $\partial_t^α$ with $α\in(0,1)$ is the Caputo derivative in time and $(-Δ)^s$ with $s\in(0,1]$ is the fractional Laplacian in space ($s<1$) or the classical Laplacian ($s=1$). We prove that the problem has at most one bounded viscosity solution, and we obtain existence as the limit of an explicit monotone finite-difference scheme, which combines the L1 discretization of the Caputo derivative with powers of the discrete Laplacian. The scheme is also used to establish regularity estimates in space and time. We then study the large-time behaviour: solutions converge, along subsequences, to stationary solutions, and we identify the limit when the initial datum coincides with the obstacle (the minimal stationary solution) or has a limit $c_*$ at infinity. In the latter case the dimension plays a role, as a consequence of the recurrence of the underlying process: the limit is the unique stationary solution with limit $c_*$ at infinity if $d>2s$, and a constant (the maximum between $c_*$ and the supremum of the obstacle) if $d\le2s$. Numerical experiments in one dimension, based on an explicit stationary solution, illustrate the convergence of the scheme and the relaxation towards the stationary profile.

15. 径流模型的自适应卡尔曼滤波及其在卡马斯特拉流域的应用#

Adaptive Kalman Filtering for Streamflow Models with Application to the Camastra Basin

作者: Mario Pezzella, Luigi Cimorelli, Luisa D’Amore, Salvatore Cuomo

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.04922v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04922v1

摘要: Accurate streamflow estimation requires a consistent representation of model and observation uncertainty. Such consistency is difficult to obtain when rainfall information is unavailable, or not temporally aligned with the discharge observations. We develop an adaptive Kalman filter for Generalized Shot Noise streamflow models, with recursive estimation of the measurement and process-noise covariances. The process-noise update includes a correction for the covariance propagated through the state dynamics, without which the recursion has no finite stationary point. With the correction, the expected increment vanishes when the mean normalized innovation squared reaches one. The filter is assessed using 37 years of daily inflow observations from the Camastra Basin in southern Italy, under two rainfall configurations. The first supplies a synthetic effective-rainfall realization as a deterministic input, whereas the second represents the unresolved forcing through the adaptive process noise. The second configuration yields the more accurate estimates, since the synthetic rainfall events, although statistically calibrated, do not coincide in time with those that generated the observed discharge. Over the full record, it attains a Nash–Sutcliffe efficiency of 0.95 and a median relative error of about 1%. Regime-conditional consistency diagnostics are reported and discussed for the second configuration.

16. 基于边缘化的正则化用于可分离非线性最小二乘问题及其在半盲去模糊中的应用#

Marginalization-Based Regularization for Separable Nonlinear Least Squares Problems with Applications to Semi-Blind Deblurring

作者: Jordan T. Dworaczyk, Malena I. Español

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.04846v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04846v1

摘要: Separable nonlinear least squares problems arise in inverse problems such as semi-blind image deblurring, where the image enters the forward model linearly, and the blur parameters enter nonlinearly. Even when the image is regularized, joint estimation of the image and blur parameters can favor a degenerate no-blur solution, in which the blur operator tends to the identity and the reconstruction merely denoises the data without removing the blur. We address this by regularizing the blur parameters. Motivated by the determinant term arising from Gaussian marginalization, we construct a family of blur-parameter priors whose weight and auxiliary operators can be chosen independently of the image prior. The resulting log-determinant penalty is incorporated into regularized variable projection, which eliminates the image variables by minimization. Under periodic boundary conditions and assuming the matrices involved admit a joint spectral decomposition, we show that a sufficiently large determinant weight rules out the no-blur solution as a local minimizer. We also characterize the regularizer’s behavior as the auxiliary regularization scale tends to zero or infinity. We solve the reduced problem with a quasi-Newton method that evaluates the objective and its derivatives in the Fourier domain. In experiments with Gaussian, defocus, and motion blur, the proposed prior improves image and blur recovery over Gaussian marginalization across several settings, with determinant weights computed from the true image. A single-sample experiment illustrates how to automatically choose parameters from the data using a conditional generalized cross-validation criterion.

17. Korteweg-de Vries 方程双曲近似的渐近保持 IMEX Active Flux 格式#

Asymptotic preserving IMEX Active Flux schemes for a hyperbolic approximation of the Korteweg-de Vries equation

作者: Simon Krotsch, Wasilij Barsukow, Christian Klingenberg

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.04832v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04832v1

摘要: We present a theoretical and numerical analysis of novel IMEX Active Flux schemes for a hyperbolic approximation of the Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) equation. For this approximation, it is important that the numerical schemes recover the limit equations (asymptotic preserving) and retain their order of accuracy in the limit (asymptotically accurate). Using formal asymptotic analysis and numerical experiments, we show that an Active Flux spatial discretisation (closely related to the standard semi-discrete Active Flux scheme), combined with an implicit-explicit Runge-Kutta scheme, is asymptotic preserving but not asymptotically accurate. We therefore propose alternative spatial discretisations that differ in the update equations of the point values. First, we derive a scheme by rescaling and eliminating terms in the upwind matrices. Second, we develop a second-order scheme by using central discretisation for the terms treated implicitly in time and present two modifications of it to third-order. Finally, we formulate a scheme with predefined upwind directions that are not chosen based on characteristics. We show that the alternative schemes are not only asymptotic preserving but also asymptotically accurate, and compare their accuracy, stability, and energy conservation numerically.

18. 矩阵多项式固定秩逼近的一般因子分解与交替方向法#

Generic factorizations and alternating direction method for fixed-rank approximation of matrix polynomials

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04799v1

摘要: We present an algorithm for approximating a given matrix polynomial of degree $d$ by a matrix polynomial of prescribed rank and degree at most $d$. The method builds on recent advances in the theory of generic eigenstructures and factorizations of matrix polynomials with bounded rank and degree. We also give a specialized treatment of the case of matrix pencils (degree one matrix polynomials), motivated by its particular theoretical and practical importance. Lastly, numerical experiments are reported to assess the performance of the proposed approach and to compare it with existing methods.

19. TriCG 和 TriMR 求解对称准定线性系统的收敛性分析#

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.04189v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04189v1

摘要: TriCG and TriMR are two short-recurrence iterative methods introduced by Montoison and Orban [SIAM J.\ Sci.\ Comput., 43 (2021), pp.\ A2502–A2525] for solving symmetric quasi-definite linear systems. 20. 使用 Rosenbrock 时间积分方法提升正则化 Stokeslet 模拟的性能#

Improving the performance of regularized Stokeslet simulations using Rosenbrock time integration methods

作者: Moslem Uddin, Lisa J. Fauci, Ricardo Cortez, Tommaso Buvoli

类别: physics.flu-dyn, math.NA, physics.bio-ph

发布日期: 2026-11-03

arXiv ID: 2611.05428v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05428v1

摘要: The method of regularized Stokeslets (MRS) is widely used to model fluid-structure interactions of microscopic bodies in viscous environments. Specifically, MRS computes the fluid velocity generated by regularized forces distributed on an immersed body, which in turn defines a system of ordinary differential equations with which to evolve the body so that the no-slip condition is enforced. To accurately model material properties such as elasticity and bending rigidity, the resulting ODE system is often stiff and, therefore, computationally expensive to solve with explicit time-integration methods. Here we demonstrate the potential of linearly implicit Rosenbrock (ROS) time integration methods to greatly reduce the computational time required to solve such ODE systems. We focus on the classic model problem of the swimming of a flexible filament driven by preferred curvature forcing. Our findings indicate that, compared to previous MRS simulations, the increased stability of ROS methods allows for speed-ups between twenty and two thousand times over explicit Runge-Kutta (ERK) methods. Moreover, we present approximate scaling laws for this speedup as a function of the key model parameters (bending rigidity and slenderness) at the root of numerical stiffness. Lastly, beyond the basic ROS approach, we discuss several additional strategies that further improve performance.

21. 互易 MIMO 端口-哈密顿动力系统的自适应数据驱动降阶模型#

Adaptive data-driven reduced-order models for reciprocal MIMO port-Hamiltonian dynamical systems

作者: Anarzhan Abilgazy, Mikhail Zaslavsky

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.04065v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04065v1

摘要: Port-Hamiltonian (pH) systems provide a general framework for modeling a broad class of physical dynamical systems, including systems with dissipation. Constructing accurate and robust data-driven reduced-order models for general pH systems is challenging. In this work, we focus on an important subclass of pH systems satisfying reciprocity, a property arising naturally in a variety of models. Rather than seeking to preserve the full pH structure, we exploit the additional symmetry provided by reciprocity to develop an efficient data-driven reduced-order modeling approach. We show that every minimal realization of a reciprocal system is self-adjoint with respect to a generally indefinite inner product and can, up to a similarity transformation preserving the pH structure, be represented in a Krein space with a signature inner product. This observation motivates a Galerkin projection onto a tangential rational Krylov subspace with respect to the corresponding indefinite inner product. Although the resulting projection involves full-order state solutions and the unknown inner product, we show that its reduced mass and stiffness matrices can be constructed directly from transfer-function data through a Loewner formulation. We then propose a greedy data-driven procedure that adaptively selects both interpolation frequencies and tangential directions. At every iteration, the frequency is chosen according to the largest transfer-function approximation error, while the tangential direction is selected from a dominant singular direction of the corresponding matrix-valued residual. Numerical experiments for wave propagation in lossy media demonstrate improved approximation efficiency compared with block adaptive interpolation. We further apply the method to spectral mass gauging of a three-dimensional propellant tank to provide an accurate estimate of the tank volume through Weyl’s law.

22. 有限体积粗粒化中的初值迹不充分性与熵核算分离#

Initial-Trace Insufficiency and Entropy-Accounting Separation in Finite-Volume Coarse Graining

作者: Antonis Polemitis, Nicholas Christakis, Dimitris Drikakis

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.04001v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04001v1

摘要: Coarse finite-volume information does not automatically define an exact pathwise closure for the restricted evolution of a fine-grid realization. We study two one-step information boundaries that are distinct from recursive predictive memory. First, we show that parent averages together with complete initial parent-interface trace pairs are sufficient for one forward-Euler finite-volume transition with a deterministic two-state numerical flux, but they do not generally determine a completed second-order strong-stability-preserving Runge-Kutta, equivalently Heun, transition. An explicit scalar upwind collision shows that hidden interior data can affect later stage traces and hence the completed parent update, even when initial traces and parent averages agree. Second, we show that exact conservative one-step closure is not the same as entropy-accounting closure. A parent entropy balance obtained from a cellwise numerical entropy balance contains a subcell Jensen gap, a realized entropy-flux difference, and residual dissipation. For a declared Burgers-Rusanov conservative flux and a declared Rusanov-type numerical entropy flux, we construct a sign-indexed finite family in which all branches have identical parent averages, identical conservative interface registers, and identical next parent averages, while the entropy-interface register distinguishes every branch. Under the uniform branch law, the conservative channel leaves nonzero deterministic squared-error floors for both the raw entropy register and its cyclic divergence. The results give a concise finite-volume information-sufficiency test for multistage interface data and entropy-side accounting.

23. 加权随机 Kaczmarz 求解稀疏损坏线性系统#

Weighted Randomized Kaczmarz for Sparsely Corrupted Linear Systems

作者: Jessie Chen, Ziyu Cheng, Yunhui He, Alona Kryshchenko, Jocelyn T. Chi, Anna Ma, Jamie Haddock

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.03889v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03889v1

摘要: In this paper, we introduce a generalized weighted randomized Kaczmarz framework to approximate solutions to overdetermined linear systems whose measurements are perturbed by sparse, additive corruptions. The proposed framework samples equations with probabilities proportional to a user-defined weighting function of their corresponding residual magnitudes. Our work focuses on weighting functions that will reduce the influence of potentially corrupted equations, and provides analyses for a general class of non-increasing weighting functions. We derive one-step expected squared error bounds that separate progress on uncorrupted equations from the contribution of corrupted equations, and provide sufficient conditions for geometric convergence under one specific family of weighting functions – threshold weighting. We also investigate adaptive exponential weights scaled by the median or median absolute deviation of residual magnitudes, requiring no prior knowledge of the corruption fraction or magnitudes. Lastly, we consider a family of inverse residual weighting functions. Numerical experiments demonstrate the benefit of such a generalized framework in its flexibility in developing approaches for settings in which information about the corruptions is known or unknown.

25. 多轨迹迭代并行优化#

Multi-Trajectory Parallel-in-Iteration Optimization

作者: Guilherme H. M. Araujo, Oliver A. Krzysik, Hans De Sterck

类别: math.NA, math.OC

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.03621v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03621v1

摘要: Previously, we developed a ``parallel-in-iteration’’ optimization framework that uses multigrid reduction-in-time (MGRIT) to parallelize slowly converging optimization methods, such as gradient descent. Unlike time-dependent evolution problems, optimization requires only a final point with a sufficiently small gradient, rather than an accurate approximation of the entire trajectory of iterates. This motivates a different use of MGRIT: instead of repeatedly improving the approximation of a single long trajectory, we introduce restarted MGRIT (R-MGRIT), which performs one MGRIT iteration on a trajectory, takes the approximate final point as a new initial guess, and repeats the process on a new trajectory. Each restart thus defines a new all-at-once system, allowing progress toward the solution without accurately approximating the preceding trajectory. Moreover, we find that these trajectories can be shortened without increasing the number of restarts needed for convergence. We provide theoretical results comparing MGRIT and R-MGRIT for linear problems and demonstrate the convergence behavior of the shortened trajectories numerically for both linear and nonlinear problems. Shortening the trajectories increases the estimated parallel speedup while reducing the number of processors required.

26. 多模态基于 Laplace 的采样#

Multi-modal Laplace-based sampling

作者: Maxime Egéa, Hanyue Gu, Daniel Rudolf, Björn Sprungk

类别: math.ST, math.NA, math.PR, stat.CO

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.03773v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03773v1

摘要: We study the multi-modal Laplace approximation, given by a weighted Gaussian mixture centred at the local minima of the negative log-density, for sampling from probability measures that concentrate around finitely many modes as an inverse-temperature parameter $n$ grows. Such distributions occur in Bayesian inference in the small noise or large data regime. We derive explicit, non-asymptotic bounds on the Hellinger distance between the multi-modal Laplace approximation and the concentrating posterior. The bound decays like $n^{-1/2}$ with $n$ and the constants depend only polynomially on the dimension $d$. Moreover, we analyse the Laplace mixture as a proposal distribution for self-normalised importance sampling and independent Metropolis–Hastings (IMH) targeting concentration multimodal distributions. We derive asymptotic results, as $n\to\infty$, on the effective sample size in case of importance sampling as well as on the average acceptance probability, lag-one autocorrelation, and spectral gap in case of IMH. 28. 面向 tempered 分数阶 CGMY 定价的降阶与神经代理模型的基于符号的残差认证#

类别: math.NA, q-fin.CP

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.03463v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03463v1

摘要: We certify reduced and neural approximations of European-put price trajectories under the Carr–Geman–Madan–Yor model by residual bounds for the spline Galerkin discretisation of the tempered-fractional pricing equation. The Galerkin matrices are Toeplitz sections of an explicit periodised Fourier symbol; summing the alias series to a stated tail makes the assembled system exact to $10^{-8}$. A Lévy–Khintchine argument gives the parameter-explicit bound $λ_{\min}(S_I)\ge r\,m_{\min}$ for the symmetric part, valid for $Y\in(0,2)\setminus\{1\}$, replacing numerical spectral estimates. A banded comparison norm built from the mass and stiffness symbols yields a rigorous certificate at $O(N)$ cost with explicit effectivity, and empirical interpolation of the symbol makes the operator affine in about 36 terms with a rigorous error transfer, so a reduced model is certified at a cost independent of $N$; a three-stage rule escalates to the full-order bounds only when needed. At the resolution that prices the whole parameter box to one cent ($N=1024$), the full-order certificate costs 6% of a direct solve, and the certified POD–Galerkin model of rank 80 costs 3% of it and settles 99.8% of $10^4$ queries without touching the full space. The neural reduced models trained here miss the one-cent tolerance by a factor of at least 46; the certificate detects this at every query in a few milliseconds and explains it as a trade-off between temporal smoothness and coefficient accuracy. A residual certificate for the American put is proved as an outlook.

摘要: Hybrid models and neural differential equations (NDEs) are becoming increasingly important for modelling physical systems; however, they often encounter stability and accuracy issues during long-term integration. Training on unrolled trajectories is known to limit these divergences but quickly becomes too computationally expensive due to the need to compute gradients over an iterative process, making this method impractical for large-scale physical systems (e.g. weather and climate forecasting). Two popular paradigms for improving the stability of learned dynamics are to train on interpolated or noisy samples or to regularize the Jacobian of the learned representation. 30. 具有两个无界方向和一个周期方向的 Poisson 问题的格点格林函数#

Lattice Green’s functions for Poisson problems with two unbounded and one periodic direction

作者: Gilles Poncelet, Philippe Chatelain

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.03458v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03458v1

摘要: Fourier-based Poisson solvers treat periodic directions spectrally and unbounded ones by the Hockney–Eastwood convolution with a free-space kernel. With two unbounded and one periodic direction, the setting of vortex tubes, wakes and jets, the kernel used so far by these solvers combines the two-dimensional kernel on the zero periodic mode with the symbol of the operator sampled on the doubled grid on every other mode. We show that the second ingredient is not exact: the error is the field of the periodic images of the source and independent of the mesh size. While the period $L_z$ is not much longer than the unbounded extent $L_x$, it is of order $\exp(-2πd/L_z)$, with $d$ the distance to the nearest image; beyond, it grows like $(L_z/L_x)^2$. The error is thus small on cubic domains, but larger than the solution itself on elongated ones. We remove the approximation by extending the integral representation of the exact second-order kernel of Hou and Colonius: the screened kernel of each mode is evaluated in closed form along one unbounded direction and by Gauss–Legendre quadrature along the other, for the high-order dimension-split and Mehrstellen stencils, at a one-off cost of about one solve. The kernels, implemented in the open-source library \flups{}, are verified by comparison with independent quadratures, by a padding test sensitive to the boundary condition enforced, and by convergence at the order of each stencil on an axially modulated tube source, on domains up to $64$ times longer than wide and up to $2.7\times10^{11}$ unknowns.

To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study of GDSW-type coarse spaces for polynomial degrees $k\ge 3$ in three dimensions, for either discretization. In 3D VEM, the face degrees of freedom (DOFs) are moments instead of point values. The kernel of the differential operator, which defines the interface values of the GDSW coarse basis functions, must be represented through these moments. The construction is therefore no longer purely algebraic and, without this correction, the coarse level loses its effect for $k\ge 3$. FEM and VEM are tested on hexahedral meshes, and VEM is additionally tested on polyhedral ones. 36. 用于模拟物理精确解的核化稳定流体方法#

Kernelized Stable Fluids for simulating physically accurate solutions

作者: L. Scholz, A. Bacho, H. Owhadi

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.03263v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03263v1

摘要: The Stable Fluids algorithm is a fractional step method for solving the incompressible Navier-Stokes (NS) equations in an unconditionally stable way. It was initially developed for visual effects and allows real-time simulations because of its low computational complexity. The method solves the advection step by using the method of characteristics which is implemented via linear interpolation. This results in excessive diffusion of vorticity and is one of the main reasons for the physical inaccuracy of the algorithm. We propose Kernelized Stable Fluids with Sparse Cholesky Preconditioning (KSF-Chol) in order to address this shortcoming and make the algorithm thereby suitable for engineering problems and scientific computing. The algorithm is modified by replacing the linear interpolation approach with kernel interpolation in the advection step. We accelerate the computationally expensive kernel interpolation by using sparse Cholesky preconditioning and a near-/far-field approach and optimize both methods for computations on a uniform Cartesian grid. KSF-Chol achieves per time step a near-linear computational complexity of $\mathcal{O}(N\log^d(N/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{chol}}))$ in space and time. We test KSF-Chol against reference solvers and numerical reference data on multiple 2D and 3D benchmarks and demonstrate a significantly reduced numerical diffusion as well as a competitive accuracy concerning the computation of relevant flow quantities.

37. FEniCS 中界面问题的多 GPU 加速及其在心脏建模中的应用#

作者: Edoardo Centofanti, Benjamin Pachev, Joshua Steyer, James D. Trotter, Pietro Benedusi

类别: math.NA, cs.DC, cs.MS

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.03181v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03181v1

摘要: GPU acceleration of finite element assembly is well established for standard formulations, but mixed-dimensional problems, in which volumetric subdomains are coupled via lower-dimensional interfaces, remain unsupported in automated frameworks such as FEniCS. We extend cuDOLFINx, a GPU-enabled extension of the DOLFINx finite element framework, to assemble such interface problems on GPUs while preserving the high-level FEniCS programming model. Combined with GPU-resident Krylov solvers and algebraic multigrid preconditioning, this enables end-to-end GPU execution of the EMI (Extracellular–Membrane–Intracellular) model, an elliptic interface problem from cardiac modeling in which many intracellular subdomains are coupled through membranes. We compare the GPU implementation against a CPU-based one through strong and weak scaling experiments on regular and realistic 3D cardiac geometries with up to 8000 cells and 22 million degrees of freedom. In terms of assembly, a single GH200 node is roughly equivalent to two 144-core CPU nodes in strong scaling, and to two to three nodes in weak scaling, depending on geometry. For the linear solver, performance depends on geometry, preconditioning, and problem scale. On the regular geometry, GPU advantages emerge mainly at larger problem sizes and node counts. On the realistic geometry, with the same algebraic multigrid preconditioner for both architectures, the GPU is faster at all tested scales. The implementation is available as open-source software and applies to other problems with interface coupling.

38. Ozaki 矩阵乘法中的自适应精度驱动分裂及其在分块 LU 分解中的应用#

Adaptive accuracy-driven splitting in Ozaki matrix multiplication and application to block LU factorization

作者: Erin Carson, Yuxin Ma

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.03158v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03158v1

摘要: The Ozaki scheme for matrix multiplication computes an accurate matrix product by splitting the input matrices and evaluating a sequence of low-precision matrix products. Its accuracy and computational cost depend strongly on the number of splits. We develop accuracy-driven criteria for selecting this number in both Ozaki matrix multiplication and in block LU factorization using Ozaki trailing updates. For matrix multiplication, we derive normwise and componentwise criteria that determine how many split products are required to achieve a given accuracy requirement. For block LU, we present a fixed normwise strategy with essentially no selection cost, an LU-aware adaptive normwise strategy that can reduce the number of splits in subsequent trailing updates, and a componentwise strategy that uses information accumulated from preceding updates to guide the later selections. In the componentwise strategy, when the required number of splits exceeds a prescribed maximum, the corresponding update is instead carried out using ordinary matrix multiplication. We also provide rigorous backward error bounds for these approaches. Numerical experiments show that the adaptive normwise strategy can reduce the number of splits while preserving normwise accuracy, whereas the componentwise strategy can provide smaller componentwise backward errors when stronger local accuracy is needed.

作者: Marc Hunkemöller, Nico Westerbeck, Joost van Dijk, Lars Schewe, Dirk Witthaut

发布日期: 2026-11-02

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03012v1

摘要: Contingency analysis repeatedly solves the power-flow equations under many single-element outages, an increasingly demanding workload as operating margins tighten and renewable variability grows. Graphics processing units (GPUs) are attractive for this task, but a change in topology alters the sparsity pattern of the Jacobian, preventing the uniform batched factorization on which efficient GPU solvers rely. This paper presents a GPU-parallel Newton–Raphson solver for $N\!-\!1$ contingency analysis that separates the change in operating state from the change in topology. A single reference Jacobian is inverted once and reused across all contingencies. Each topology change is expressed as a low-rank Woodbury correction, while the residual state-dependent perturbation is handled by an inner iterative solve, a truncated Neumann series or the biconjugate gradient stabilized method. We analyze the spectral radius and condition number of the resulting iteration matrices and show that the reference Jacobian is an effective preconditioner. On transmission-scale benchmarks, the method attains per-topology solve times competitive with an optimized 80-core central processing unit baseline, with the low-rank correction accounting for approximately 1–2% of the workload. This makes GPU-parallel $N\!-\!1$ contingency analysis feasible at transmission scale without per-topology refactorization.

40. 弱可压缩 SPH 声学项中计算性能与数值精度的比较#

Computational performance versus numerical accuracy in the acoustic terms of weakly-compressible SPH

作者: Giuseppe Bilotta, Alireza Zarei, Roberta Cristofaro, Alexis Hérault, Élie Saikali, Morteza Derakhti

类别: math.NA, math-ph, physics.comp-ph, physics.flu-dyn

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.02994v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02994v1

摘要: Accurate computation of the acoustic terms in weakly-compressible SPH, including the equation of state, its inverse, and the evaluation of the local speed of sound, is essential to ensure energy conservation, robust solid boundary conditions, and correct open boundary behavior. In the classic Cole’s equation of state, using values of the polytropic constant $γ\ne 1$ can lead to significant precision loss, particularly when computations are performed in single precision, as is common in GPU implementations. We extend here our previous results on the optimal choice of state variable by introducing a new higher-precision form for all three acoustic terms, denoted \texttt{explog}, and assess its numerical accuracy and computational performance across a variety of devices and test cases. We find that the new form evaluates the acoustic terms with a maximum ULP of~2 for arguments less than $0.125$, and has higher errors in less than 1% of the arguments larger than the threshold. Computationally, the new formulation is also faster than the naive one on GPU, and only marginally slower on CPU.

41. 耦合扩散系统稳定约化的信息极限#

Information Limits for Stable Reduction of Coupled Diffusion Systems

作者: Hiroki Ishizaka

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.02977v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02977v1

摘要: Eliminating an unresolved diffusion field from a coupled parabolic system produces an operator-valued memory term and a contribution from the hidden initial state. We study whether a reduced prediction of the retained field meets a prescribed tolerance. For known hidden initial data, the discarded response, damped by diffusion and transmitted through the coupling, gives an upper error bound; an auxiliary diffusion problem supplies a lower bound. Rigorous discretisation bounds then yield tests that accept, reject or remain undecided. If the discarded initial state is known only through a norm bound, the minimum worst-case error equals that bound multiplied by the time-uniform gain from the missing state to the retained trajectory. Our main result encloses this infinite-dimensional gain using a finite spectral Galerkin evolution, its residual in the full equations and an explicit diffusion tail. The enclosure converges without compactness or smallness of the coupling. Strict comparisons of the optimal radius with a tolerance have finite certificates if all endpoint quantities admit arbitrarily accurate rigorous enclosures. Multiplication-coupling examples with infinitely many dynamically coupled modes illustrate both settings, including explicit finite-element error budgets.

43. 基于 Legendre 的改进极限学习机用于对流扩散方程：稳态、连续时间与离散时间形式#

A Legendre-Based Modified Extreme Learning Machine for Convection-Diffusion Equations: Steady-State, Continuous-Time, and Discrete-Time Formulations

作者: Gadade Navnath Ankush, Ram Shiromani, Tomas Gandara, Ramon Codina, Sivaram Ambikasaran

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.02800v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02800v1

摘要: Convection-diffusion equations are fundamental models for transport phenomena, but their numerical solution becomes challenging in convection-dominated regimes due to steep gradients and sharp boundary layers. This motivates the development of accurate approaches capable of effectively resolving such features while avoiding costly iterative optimization. In this study, a modified ELM framework based on shifted Legendre polynomials is developed for convection-diffusion equations in steady-state, continuous-time, and discrete-time formulations. The proposed approach is evaluated on steady and unsteady benchmark problems with relatively high Peclet numbers, including continuous and discontinuous boundary conditions. Finite element method solutions are used as independent solution references. By exploiting the linearity of the governing equations, MELM determines the output weights through a single least-squares solve, avoiding the iterative gradient-based training required by PINNs. The shifted Legendre polynomial basis provides an effective spectral representation for resolving steep gradients and sharp boundary layers. For the DT-MELM formulation, the coefficient matrix remains unchanged across successive time levels, allowing its LU or QR factorization to be computed once and reused. A systematic comparison with PINNs that incorporate advanced training strategies demonstrates that MELM provides higher accuracy and shorter solution times for steady-state and DT-MELM formulations across the benchmark problems considered. In contrast, CT-MELM exhibits scalability limitations as the size of the space–time basis increases. Overall, the results demonstrate the effectiveness of MELM for steady-state and discrete-time convection–diffusion problems, while also showing that PINNs provide better scalability for large space–time formulations where global CT-MELM becomes computationally demanding.

44. POD-CNF：基于归一化流与本征正交分解的参数化随机偏微分方程降阶建模#

POD-CNF: reduced order modeling of parametrized stochastic PDEs via normalizing flows and proper orthogonal decomposition

作者: Andrea Violante, Nicola Rares Franco, Andrea Manzoni

类别: math.NA, cs.LG

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.02787v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02787v1

摘要: Reduced-order models (ROMs) learned from trusted numerical simulations enable the real-time simulation of complex physical systems, making computationally prohibitive tasks-such as uncertainty quantification and optimal control-tractable. In particular, data-driven ROMs are opening the opportunity to learn from both numerical simulations and experimental data. However, integrating these sources of information is far from straightforward. Real-world measurements often involve multiple sources of uncertainty, including non-Gaussian noise, latent heterogeneities, unknown or hidden system parameters, and multiscale phenomena occurring below the resolution of the data. Consequently, real-world data are better described by parametrized stochastic partial differential equations, for which the development of effective ROMs remains relatively unexplored. In this work, we introduce POD-CNF, a data-driven generative ROM for efficient uncertainty quantification of parametrized high-dimensional systems. The approach combines projection-based model reduction and deep generative modeling. First, Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) is employed to obtain a low-dimensional representation of the solution manifold. A Conditional Normalizing Flow (CNF) is then trained to approximate the density of the reduced coefficients conditionally on the input parameters. This formulation allows the surrogate to capture complex, multimodal output distributions, going beyond standard regression-based ROMs and least-squares predictions. We assess the effectiveness of POD-CNF through numerical experiments, demonstrating its ability to accurately reproduce output distributions while substantially reducing computational costs. Finally, a proof-of-concept application illustrates how the generative paradigm facilitates integration into Bayesian inverse problems, highlighting promising directions for future research.

46. 线性系统的两点残量步长梯度法#

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02743v1

摘要: Kaczmarz-type methods often converge slowly when the coefficient matrix is ill-conditioned. To break this slow-convergence regime, we borrow the delayed-step-size idea of the Barzilai-Borwein (BB) method and, within the framework of the residual-based surrogate-hyperplane Kaczmarz method, propose two new step sizes for gradient-descent-type methods solving linear systems: a delayed residual step size (α_k^{\mathrm{DR}}) and a residual-difference step size (α_k^{\mathrm{RD}}), which correspond, respectively, to the BB1 and BB2 step sizes in convex quadratic optimization. 47. 扰动傅里叶采样中的混叠几何与密度补偿#

Alias Geometry and Density Compensation in Perturbed Fourier Sampling

作者: Erli Wind-Andersen

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-02

arXiv ID: 2611.02379v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02379v1

摘要: On a uniform grid with equal weights, Fourier modes that remain distinct on the grid are exactly orthogonal. When the nodes are perturbed, density compensation readjusts the weights. We ask how much of the resulting error reweighting can remove, measuring it by the Frobenius norm of the Gram defect, whose square is proportional to the mean-square error of density-compensated adjoint reconstruction. The answer is governed by aliasing. The grid cannot distinguish certain frequency differences, so a weight correction must treat each such alias class as a single number, while a node displacement affects every frequency difference in the class separately. To first order, reweighting removes the class average but not the spread. The irreducible error is a sum of covariance matrices, one for each class of colliding frequency differences. If no differences collide, every small node perturbation can be repaired exactly with positive weights. With collisions, a repair that works to first order can fail at second order. At the two-dimensional DFT, row and column shears are each exactly repairable, but their combination is not, and we compute the obstruction. In one dimension, oversampling protects a band of low-frequency node motions, and under random jitter optimal reweighting asymptotically removes a fraction $1-κ^2(2-κ)^2$ of the expected squared first-order error at oversampling ratio $κ\in[1,2]$. At critical sampling, node and weight perturbations are orthogonal: no reweighting helps, Voronoi weights can never reduce the error, and for the one-dimensional DFT the remaining error is a log-Vandermonde energy with the circular $H^{1/2}$ energy as its continuum limit.

48. Ehrlich–Aberth 求根法并非普遍收敛#

The Ehrlich–Aberth root finder is not generally convergent

作者: Torben Grabbel, Nedim Srkalovic, Dierk Schleicher

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-01

arXiv ID: 2611.02143v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02143v1

摘要: We show that the well-known Ehrlich–Aberth root finding method is not generally convergent. More precisely, we show that there is an open subset of polynomials of any degree $d\ge 7$, and an open subset of initial conditions, such that the iteration of the Ehrlich–Aberth method converges to an attracting periodic cycle of period $4$, rather than to a vector of roots. The proof is constructive in the sense that we present explicit values for the polynomial and the $4$-cycle. The search is highly non-trivial: we found no attracting cycles for lower degrees/periods, and the size of the attracting domain relative to a ``unit cube’’ in the space of polynomials and starting vectors is on the order of $10^{-127}$.

49. 通过张量分解加速晶粒长大的相场模拟#

Accelerating phase-field simulations of grain growth via tensor decomposition

作者: Chaoqian Yuan, Dingxiang Zhu, Ye Lu

类别: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-01

摘要: Phase-field models provide a powerful framework for simulating grain growth phenomena in polycrystalline materials, but their application to large-scale systems is limited by the high computational and memory costs associated with resolving multiple phase variables over fine spatial and temporal scales. This paper presents a tensor decomposition based model reduction framework for accelerating phase-field simulations of grain growth. The proposed method exploits the underlying low-rank structure of the evolving phase-field variables and represents the high-dimensional solution in a spatially decomposed tensor format. By performing the numerical computations directly within this reduced representation, the framework decreases storage requirements and reduces the cost of repeatedly solving the phase-field equations. 50. Biot 方程稳定化控制体积离散的参数鲁棒预条件子#

作者: Lei Wei, Lina Zhao

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-01

arXiv ID: 2611.01922v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01922v1

摘要: We develop and analyze parameter-robust block preconditioners for a stabilized control-volume discretization of the quasi-static Biot equations. Beginning with a five-field formulation, we eliminate the poroelastic stress and time-scaled Darcy velocity through independent vertex-local solves, obtaining a symmetric cell-centered displacement–rotation–pressure system. A macrocell rotation-jump term stabilizes the weak-symmetry constraint. We prove the well-posedness of the fully discrete scheme and show that the condensed, coupled displacement–rotation mechanics block is uniformly spectrally equivalent to its block-diagonal counterpart. For uncoupled preconditioners, we derive an exact discrete pressure-control criterion and a sufficient parameter condition that accommodates the scaling (c_0\asymp K_{\rm dr}^{-1}), thereby allowing the storage coefficient to vanish in the nearly incompressible limit. We then construct a Schur-consistent pressure block that approximates the mechanics-induced pressure correction and prove its uniform equivalence to the exact pressure Schur complement. These results yield parameter-independent spectral bounds for block-diagonal preconditioning and weighted field-of-values estimates for lower-triangular preconditioning. Practical realizations employ a macrocell mass-matrix approximation for the stabilized rotation block and tailored two-grid solvers for the displacement and pressure blocks. Numerical experiments verify the expected convergence behavior in two dimensions and demonstrate robust performance under mesh refinement and broad variations of the physical parameters in both two and three dimensions.

51. 流形上设计曲线的最优渐近界#

Optimal asymptotic bounds for design curves on manifolds

作者: Martin Ehler

类别: math.MG, math.CO, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-01

arXiv ID: 2611.01895v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01895v1

摘要: A spherical $t$-design is a finite set of points on the $d$-dimensional unit sphere such that averaging any polynomial of degree at most $t$ over these points gives its exact spherical average. We study the corresponding notion for curves, where point averages are replaced by normalized arclength integration. For fixed $d\ge 2$, the length of such curves is known to be bounded below by a positive multiple of $t^{d-1}$ as $t\to\infty$. We show that this asymptotic order is attained: for every $d\ge 2$, there exists a constant $C_d>0$ such that, for every positive integer $t$ and every prescribed length $L\ge C_dt^{d-1}$, there is a simple spherical $t$-design curve of length $L$. This establishes the analogue for curves of the theorem of Bondarenko, Radchenko, and Viazovska, which resolved the Korevaar-Meyers conjecture for spherical design points. More generally, we prove the corresponding result on closed, connected Riemannian manifolds of dimension $d\ge 2$, where eigenfunctions of the Laplace-Beltrami operator substitute for polynomials. The proof combines a geometric construction of curves with continuously adjustable local lengths and an application of Brouwer’s degree theorem.

52. 基于 ZX 演算的张量缩并：一种图论方法#

Tensor Contraction with ZX Calculus, a Graph-Theoretic Approach

作者: Yuhao Han

类别: quant-ph, math-ph, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-11-01

arXiv ID: 2611.01890v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01890v1

摘要: Tensors are higher dimensional generalisations of vectors and matrices. Tensor networks are a diagrammatic representation of complicated relations between tensors, and tensor contraction is the evaluation of a tensor network analogous to matrix multiplication. Tensor networks and tensor contraction are ubiquitous in computer science, physics, and applied mathematics. Most existing tensor contraction algorithms are focused on finding an optimal contraction order of the tensors’ bonds. However, finding the optimal order is an NP-hard problem. It is equivalent to another possibly more straightforward but still NP-hard problem of finding the treewidth of the line graph of the tensor network. While in general finding the optimal bond-by-bond contraction order is hard, in many practical cases there is no need to find an optimal order. This observation leads to the core innovation of this thesis, which is a new tensor contraction paradigm that uses graph theoretic operations to produce an equivalent tensor network whose line graph has lower treewidth than the original tensor network, before applying the bond-by-bond contraction algorithm. We call these graph theoretical operations \textit{rewrite preprocessing}, and we name the algorithms that apply rewrite preprocessing before regular bond-by-bond contraction \textit{Tensor Contraction with Graph Operation}. There are many possible ways to perform rewrite preprocessing; in this thesis, we focus on the graph operations of ZX calculus, a diagrammatic language for quantum circuits. Ample numerical simulations have been performed to demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.

53. 用于低秩矩阵恢复的黎曼高斯-牛顿法的秩最优样本复杂度#

摘要: In this paper, we study the recovery of an unknown rank-$r$ matrix $\boldsymbol{X}_\star \in \mathbb{R}^{n_1\times n_2}$ from Gaussian linear measurements using the Riemannian Gauss-Newton (RGN) algorithm. Each iteration of RGN solves the RGN equation followed by a fixed-rank retraction. We prove that, with high probability, RGN with spectral initialization converges quadratically to $\boldsymbol{X}_\star$ using $\mathcal{O} ( r(n_1+n_2)κ^2 )$ measurements, where $κ$ is the condition number of $\boldsymbol{X}_\star$. The key to the proof is a sharp spectral-norm analysis of the iterates, which requires controlling the inverse operator arising from the RGN equation. Extensive numerical experiments support our theoretical findings and demonstrate the advantages of RGN.

摘要: We develop an incidence-based edge-averaged method for scalar convection-diffusion problems on convex polytopal meshes in arbitrary dimensions. 59. 加权变差空间中的最小范数插值：正问题与反问题#

Minimum-Norm Interpolation in Weighted Variation Spaces: Direct and Inverse Problems

作者: Weizhao Li, Lei Shi, Hanfei Zhou

类别: math.NA, stat.ML

发布日期: 2026-10-31

arXiv ID: 2611.01105v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01105v1

摘要: We study minimum-norm interpolation in weighted variation spaces generated by ReLU$^s$ ridge functions on the unit ball, where $s$ is a positive integer. For a target $f$ in the weighted variation space and a node set with sufficiently small fill distance $h$, every minimum-norm interpolant satisfies the direct bound $\|f-\mathscr{I}_{X_h}f\|_{L_2(\mathbb{B}^d)}\lesssim_{d,s}h^{(2s+1)/4}\|f\|_{\mathcal{V}_s}$. Under a boundary separation condition on the nodes, we construct a matching lower bound for the worst-case interpolation error. For the inverse problem, we introduce dual certificates and derive an estimate analogous to a Bernstein inequality. Under suitable upper bounds on dictionary size and spectral lower bounds for the atomic Gram matrices, sufficiently fast $L_2$ convergence of minimum-norm interpolants along geometrically decreasing fill distances implies that the target belongs to $\mathcal{V}_s$. As a byproduct, the best $L_2$ approximation error $E_n(f)_{L_2(\mathbb{B}^d)}$ for shallow ReLU$^s$ networks of width at most $n$ satisfies $E_n(f)_{L_2(\mathbb{B}^d)}\lesssim_{d,s}n^{-1/2-(2s+1)/(2d)}\|f\|_{\mathcal{V}_s}$, with the weighted atomic cost of an approximating network bounded by a constant multiple of $\|f\|_{\mathcal{V}_s}$, independently of $n$.

60. Calderón 逆电导率问题的替代 Tikhonov 型正则化#

作者: Pavel B. Dubovski, Charles Morehead

类别: math.NA, math-ph

发布日期: 2026-10-31

arXiv ID: 2611.01034v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01034v1

摘要: This paper investigates the application of two regularization methods, classical Tikhonov and Alternative Tikhonov regularization, to the Calderón inverse conductivity problem. We develop an iterative reconstruction scheme based on linearizing the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map and approximating conductivity updates using Gaussian basis functions. The resulting linear systems are solved using both regularization methods, with particular attention given to the derivation and numerical implementation of Alternative Tikhonov regularization. We compare the performance of these methods through numerical experiments on a synthetic conductivity containing two localized regions of differing conductivity. 61. d 维三角剖分区域的闭式高斯混合表示#

作者: Ali Darijani, Benedikt Stratmann, Uwe D. Hanebeck, Ratan Bahadur Thapa, Jürgen Beyerer, Nehal Afifi

发布日期: 2026-10-31

arXiv ID: 2611.00922v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00922v1

摘要: We derive a Gaussian Mixture (GM) representation of a full-dimensional subset of Euclidean space with finite volume from a finite simplicial triangulation in closed form. On each simplex, we replace the uniform probability density function (PDF) with a Gaussian PDF that minimizes the Kullback–Leibler (KL) divergence, arriving at the information projection onto the family of Gaussian PDFs. By exploiting affine isomorphisms between simplices, the joint convexity and bijection invariance properties of the KL divergence, identities for Dirichlet PDFs, and a partition-of-unity argument, we establish a dimension-dependent but geometry-independent sharp upper bound on the global KL divergence between the resulting GM PDF and the uniform PDF on the original domain. Numerical examples illustrate the construction and demonstrate its potential in practical applications.

62. 一种用于天线阵列分析的带局部响应压缩与群对称约化的聚类等效源方法#

作者: Guoqing Zhao, Liang Li, Xing Li, Hao Kang

类别: math.NA, math-ph

发布日期: 2026-10-31

arXiv ID: 2611.00914v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00914v1

摘要: Full-wave analysis of finite antenna arrays is computationally demanding because detailed element responses must be resolved and coupled across the entire array. To reduce this cost, this paper presents a clustered equivalent-source method that combines local response compression with group-symmetry reduction. Local responses are represented in compressed equivalent-surface bases or coordinates inherited from child clusters. Symmetry constraints associated with the cluster partition and excitation reduce the coupled system, while the corresponding group transformations recover the complete response in the retained coordinates. To assess overall efficiency, we evaluate the costs of local construction, propagation, global solution, and recovery. Numerical examples involving layered and finite annular patch arrays show close agreement between the computed and reference complex far fields and shorter complete-run times than those of the corresponding symmetry-reduced direct solvers. Among the three equivalent-source implementations for the layered array, the one with the smallest coupled system has the longest runtime because local construction and propagation dominate the cost. These findings highlight the need to consider local construction and coupling costs when assessing the benefit of reducing the global system dimension.

63. 基于 Richardson 外推的二阶随机动态模态分解#

A Second-Order Stochastic Dynamic Mode Decomposition by Richardson Extrapolation

作者: Yuanchao Xu

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-31

arXiv ID: 2611.00793v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00793v1

摘要: Stochastic dynamic mode decomposition (SDMD) approximates the Koopman semigroup of an Itô diffusion by a Galerkin generator matrix on a dictionary of observables, computed from samples of the invariant law and evaluations of the generator, and advances observables with a first-order stochastic Taylor step (SDMD-1). The second-order Taylor step (SDMD-2) also needs the matrix of second generator actions, hence a dictionary with four derivatives, evaluations of a fourth-order differential operator at every sample, and a third matrix. We show that the Richardson extrapolation of two SDMD-1 half steps (SDMD-R) is second-order accurate with the data, the matrices, and the regularity of SDMD-1. One error bound covers exact and empirical Galerkin matrices. It consists of the Galerkin error of the dictionary, which is common to all schemes, a time-stepping term, and a data term of order $m^{-1/2}$ in the sample size $m$. The time-stepping term is $O(\dt^2)$ for SDMD-R and $O(Θ_N\dt+\dt^2)$ for SDMD-2, where $Θ_N$ is the norm of the second-order Galerkin residual $\Proj_N\cA(I-\Proj_N)\cA|_{\cV_N}$. The first-order term is attained and, for self-adjoint generators, vanishes only for generator-invariant dictionaries. For reversible periodic diffusions and trigonometric dictionaries with cutoff $K$, the step restriction $\dt\lesssim K^{-2}$ makes all three schemes contractions, and the error against the Koopman semigroup on $H^\ell$ observables is $O(K^{-(\ell-2)}+\dt^p)$ plus a sampling term of order $K^{d+2}m^{-1/2}$ in dimension $d$, with $p=1$ for SDMD-1 and $p=2$ for SDMD-R and SDMD-2. Numerical experiments confirm that SDMD-R matches or improves on the accuracy of SDMD-2 at the cost of SDMD-1.

64. 重复矩阵-向量乘法的无偏低秩逼近：稳定性与最优采样#

Unbiased Low-Rank Approximation for Repeated Matrix-Vector Multiplication: Stability and Optimal Sampling

作者: Kang Liu

类别: stat.ML, cs.LG, math.NA, math.OC, math.RA

发布日期: 2026-10-31

arXiv ID: 2611.00787v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00787v1

摘要: Replacing the matrix in an iteration with random low-rank matrices can reduce the cost of matrix-vector multiplication. However, even if the sampled matrices equal the original matrix in expectation, the expected squared norm of the random iterates may grow while the original iteration converges. Under a fixed rank bound, we study how to choose the distribution of these random matrices to minimize this growth when sampling independently at each step. We derive an exact formula for the optimal asymptotic growth factor and use it to determine when the expected squared norm can converge to zero. We then characterize precisely when a fixed sampling distribution achieves this optimal factor and construct finite-support optimal distributions in every attainable case. These results determine the minimum rank required for stability and provide optimal sampling designs under the prescribed rank constraint.

65. 等熵两相流模型的通量向量分裂与半隐式有限体积格式#

Flux vector splittings and semi-implicit finite volume schemes for an isentropic two-phase flow model

作者: Michael Dumbser, Gabriella Puppo, Alessandra Spilimbergo, Tommaso Tenna

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-31

arXiv ID: 2611.00781v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00781v1

摘要: We present two new semi-implicit finite volume schemes for the 1D hyperbolic and thermodynamically compatible (HTC) isentropic two-phase flow model of Romenski et al., written in terms of mixture density, volume fraction, partial gas density, mixture momentum and the relative phase velocity. In gas-liquid mixtures, the sound speed of the weakly compressible liquid is much larger than the gas sound speed and flow velocity, so the time step of fully explicit discretizations is dictated by liquid acoustic waves, even when the interest lies in the dynamics of the gas. To overcome this difficulty, the model’s flux is split at the continuous level into an explicit subsystem (gas dynamics) and an implicit subsystem (liquid acoustic waves), leading to a nonlinear system for liquid pressure solved with the Newton method. In the first splitting the entire liquid phase is treated implicitly, while in the second the liquid convective terms are moved to the explicit subsystem, so only liquid acoustic waves are treated implicitly and nested Picard iterations are avoided. Thus, the time step of both schemes is restricted only by flow velocities and gas sound speed, independent of liquid sound speed. We prove and numerically confirm that both schemes exactly preserve the Abgrall condition (uniform velocity and pressure across material interfaces). For well-prepared initial data, both schemes are consistent with the volume balance of the liquid’s low Mach number limit. Riemann problems, pressure equilibrium tests and acoustic tests show that in non-stiff regimes the semi-implicit schemes reproduce the results of a fully explicit scheme, while in the stiff regime they allow time steps that are up to 3-4 orders of magnitude larger, avoiding the excessive numerical dissipation of explicit schemes whose numerical viscosity is dictated by liquid sound speed.

66. 球面上线性化 ReLU$^k$ 网络的参数界与逆逼近#
