类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-31

arXiv ID: 2611.00656v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00656v1

摘要: We study direct and inverse approximation for linearized shallow ReLU$^k$ networks on $\mathbb S^d$ with quasi-uniform hidden parameters. For $0<α<\frac{d+2k+1}{2d}$, under the coefficient growth condition $\bigl(n\sum_{j=1}^na_{j,n}^2\bigr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq n^αM$, we prove that approximation at the order $n^{-\frac{d+2k+1}{2d}+α}$ is equivalent to membership in the parity-compatible Besov space $\mathcal B_{2,\infty,*}^{\frac{d+2k+1}{2}-αd}(\mathbb S^d)$. Mere convergence under the same coefficient budget does not imply this regularity. For unconstrained approximation along a nested parameter sequence, a single ridge function gives a counterexample when $α<\frac{1}{2}$, whereas for $α>\frac{1}{2}$ the endpoint Besov inverse conclusion follows from a Bernstein inequality under antipodal quasi-uniformity. The inverse conclusion is also recovered when the prescribed rate holds for two quasi-uniform parameter sequences whose union is antipodally separated.

67. ILU 三角求解的并行迭代方法比较#

A comparison of parallel iterative methods for ILU triangular solves

类别: math.NA

arXiv ID: 2611.00552v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00552v1

摘要: Exact triangular solves parallelise poorly, limited by the size and number of the level sets, so on GPUs the factors from incomplete LU (ILU) factorisations are often solved approximately with parallel iterative methods. We compare four such methods: a Jacobi method, an Incomplete Sparse Approximate Inverse (ISAI), GMRES polynomials, and AIRG, a reduction multigrid built on GMRES polynomials, for which we introduce a parallel coarse/fine splitting that bounds the diagonal dominance ratio of the fine-fine block. We test them with ILU(0) and ILU(1) factors of canonical PDE problems and 91 SuiteSparse/Matrix Market matrices, the latter on a single GPU. AIRG is the most robust on the PDE factors, with nearly flat iteration counts under refinement and no per-problem tuning, but has the largest setup and memory costs. GMRES polynomials are more robust than the Jacobi method at a similar cost. As the inner solve of an ILU preconditioner with tuned inner iteration counts, ISAI gives the largest median speedups over a hipSPARSE exact triangular solve (4.5$\times$ and 6.8$\times$ for ILU(0) and ILU(1)), though this was dominated by smaller problems; on the largest ILU(1) problems all four methods give similar median speedups of 2.1-2.6$\times$.

68. 通过匹配速度学习演化#

Learning to evolve by matching velocities

作者: Nicola Farenga, Andrea Manzoni

类别: cs.LG, cs.AI, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2611.00448v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00448v1

摘要: Learning the continuous-time evolution of nonlinear dynamical systems relies on trajectory-based objectives, which require simulating the learned dynamics during training and backpropagating through the numerical scheme, at a cost that grows with the rollout horizon. We show that trajectory-based objectives degenerate as the rollout horizon increases, hindering gradient-based optimization, and that the numerical scheme biases the learned dynamics by constraining the spectrum of the learned Jacobian to its absolute stability region. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Velocity Matching (VM), a simulation-free objective that relies on piecewise-linear interpolation and stochastic collocation, and approximates the velocity population risk to second order in the sampling step. We develop a theoretical framework for velocity-based learning, introducing stability estimates that control trajectory errors in terms of velocity errors, which enable us to establish universal and continuous-time approximation results when the learned velocity field is parameterized by deep neural networks. Building on a bias-variance decomposition of the VM objective, we show that its higher-order approximation of the velocity population risk yields a consistent minimizer; that it relates to the residual of an A-stable continuous-stage Runge-Kutta scheme, thus connecting VM to implicit integration without requiring any implicit solve; and that it implicitly matches the action of the learned Jacobian to that of the true one along the observed trajectories, without requiring access to the underlying velocity field or its Jacobian. Numerical experiments on nonlinear ordinary differential equations and semi-discretized time-dependent partial differential equations show that VM improves accuracy over trajectory-based objectives, while avoiding both the cost of simulation and the instabilities of long rollouts.

69. 低精度浮点运算的兴起#

The Rise of Low Precision Floating Point

作者: Guy Lemieux, Michael L. Overton

类别: cs.MS, cs.AR, cs.LG, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2611.00301v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00301v1

摘要: Floating point computing has dominated scientific computations since the 1950s because of its versatile ability to approximate real numbers with both sufficiently high precision and sufficiently great range. Since 1985, two specific formats have become standard across the industry, one with 32 bits which is adequate for many purposes and one with 64 bits which allows numbers with both much higher precision and much greater range for applications where these are needed. However, during the past decade, deep learning has come to dominate computation, and narrower floating point formats with lower precision and reduced range have steadily become more common. In this article we discuss this phenomenon, why it is happening, how various 16-bit, 8-bit and even 4-bit floating point formats have been developed, and efforts to standardize these formats.

70. 求解带随机系数的箱约束椭圆最优控制问题的预条件半光滑牛顿法#

A Preconditioned Semismooth Newton Method for Box-Constrained Elliptic Optimal Control Problems with Random Coefficients

作者: Zhendong Li, Akwum Onwunta, Hailing Wang

类别: math.OC, math.NA, math.SP

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2611.00246v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00246v1

摘要: We consider the optimal control problem of an elliptic PDE with random diffusion under pointwise box constraints on the control. The constraints make the optimality conditions nonsmooth, with an active set that is not known in advance, and the random diffusion couples the state and the adjoint across all stochastic modes. To design a solution algorithm for this problem, we rely on the Fenchel duality viewpoint. Fenchel duality absorbs the box constraint into the objective and yields an exact reduced problem in the elliptic adjoint variable alone. After a stochastic Galerkin–finite element discretization, eliminating the state leaves a strongly monotone, piecewise smooth equation, which we solve directly by a matrix-free semismooth Newton method with the box projection retained exactly and with no outer iteration, penalty or smoothing parameter. Next, we introduce and analyze a current-set preconditioner, which adds the active-set correction of the current Newton step to a mean-diffusion reference operator and is inverted exactly by a rank-1 update. For implementation, we use a cheaper preconditioner that keeps only a fraction of free control values. Numerical experiments show second-order convergence of the state, and Newton and PCG counts that stay bounded under mesh refinement and growth of the stochastic dimension.

71. 用于多资产结构化产品高阶希腊值与敞口尾部的函数张量列#

Functional Tensor Trains for High-Order Greeks and Exposure Tails of Multi-Asset Structured Products

作者: Javier Lopez-Piqueres, Atithi Acharya, Yue Sun, Niraj Kumar

类别: q-fin.CP, math.NA, quant-ph

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.42119v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42119v1

摘要: Risk management of worst-of autocallables needs price sensitivities (Greeks) up to third order and the potential future exposure, a tail quantile of the note’s future value. In Monte Carlo, each order of differentiation multiplies the sampling noise by a factor that grows with the observation frequency, and nested simulation turns the noise of its inner paths into a bias of the quantile. We instead solve the backward pricing recursion, storing the continuation value as an $hp$ functional tensor train (hp-FTT) whose piecewise-polynomial cores are split at the barriers, so that worst-of barrier events act exactly, with rank one. For Gaussian transitions we show that the polynomial degree sufficient for an error $\varepsilon$ grows only as $\sqrt{\log(1/\varepsilon)}$. One solve then gives every Greek at any spot without sampling noise, by integrating the likelihood-ratio weight against the stored value, and the values it stores at future dates replace inner simulation. Against the strongest Monte Carlo estimators we could build or find published, on worst-of autocallables observed monthly on three to five assets, the order of the Greek and the accuracy decide which method is cheaper. At $1\%$ accuracy Monte Carlo is more than an order of magnitude cheaper for deltas, second-order Greeks go either way, and hp-FTT is cheaper for every third-order Greek, by up to two orders of magnitude. At $0.1\%$ hp-FTT is cheaper for every Greek we could check, under geometric Brownian motion and local volatility alike. For the exposure alone Monte Carlo is cheaper at $1\%$ and hp-FTT at $0.1\%$, and a solve run for the Greeks yields it for little more than scenario sampling. Sampling pays in variance for each derivative and hp-FTT in rank for each asset, so Monte Carlo keeps low-order Greeks at percent accuracy, rarely observed contracts and many assets.

72. 随机薄膜方程的 Dirichlet 型方法#

Dirichlet form approach to stochastic thin-film equations

作者: Benjamin Gess, Felix Otto, Max Sauerbrey

类别: math.PR, math.AP, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.42086v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42086v1

摘要: The thin-film equation describes the evolution of a thin liquid film driven by capillarity and limited by viscosity. For sufficiently small film heights, thermal fluctuations may not be neglected. Since the thin-film equation as an overdamped evolution equation has a gradient flow structure, the informal implementation of the fluctuation-dissipation principle is straightforward. In this paper, we work out that the concept of Dirichlet forms and the related construction of Markov processes are tailor-made for this type of overdamped continuum evolution with thermal noise. However, the concept of Dirichlet forms relies on the availability of a Gibbsian equilibrium measure, which, in case of the thin-film equation, limits the treatment to the $(1+1)$-dimensional case. By means of Dirichlet form theory, we obtain the existence and uniqueness of the evolution of laws on configuration space. We show that the regularity of the realizations of this Markov process matches the heuristics. In a simplified situation with a Gaussian reference measure and a non-degenerate mobility function, we characterize it as what is called an energy solution. The latter provides a pathwise decomposition of the driving forces into a deterministic and a thermal one, which was the starting point. Applying the same techniques in the spatially discretized setting proposed in [Math. Comp. 92 (2023), 1931–1976], we obtain the same results uniformly in the discretization parameter. In particular, Galerkin as well as spectral approximations of the stochastic thin-film equation are shown to be uniformly tight in optimal topologies. In the simplified setting, we also prove Mosco convergence of the associated Dirichlet forms and thereby obtain convergence of these approximations.

76. 量子器件中光泵浦的高保真模拟与降阶建模#

摘要: Spatially resolved modeling of optically pumped alkali-metal vapors requires the solution of a high-dimensional nonlinear diffusive quantum master equation (DQME) coupled to state-dependent light propagation. The high-fidelity solver combines second-order finite differences, explicit pseudo-time marching, and an exponential-integrator discretization of the light-propagation equation. The scheme preserves the density-matrix trace, while a derived global pseudo-time-step restriction guarantees nonnegative, bound-preserving population updates and frozen-coefficient $1$-norm stability. 77. 将基于采样的量子对角化推广到线性方程组#

Extending Sample-based Quantum Diagonalization to Systems of Linear Equations

作者: Taha Selim

类别: quant-ph, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41708v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41708v1

摘要: Solving systems of linear equations, $Ax=b$, is a ubiquitous task in computational science. The Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd algorithm requires phase estimation, controlled rotations, and post-selection, impractical on noisy intermediate-scale quantum hardware. We extend sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD), developed for quantum chemistry, to linear systems. Following the sample-based Krylov variant, the Hermitian matrix $A$ is decomposed in the Pauli basis, the Trotterized time evolution of $|b\rangle$ is sampled at several times to select computational-basis configurations, and the system is solved classically by a Galerkin projection onto the selected subspace. We prove that the subspace solution is exact when the sampled configurations contain the Krylov support of $b$ and optimal in the energy norm for symmetric positive-definite matrices, so that noise-induced configurations cannot degrade it as long as they do not displace the ideal ones. On IBM Quantum hardware, the sampled distribution of a $4\times4$ example deviates from the exact one by eleven times the shot-noise level, yet the solution is recovered exactly. For a localized problem of dimension $N=1024$, SQD selects 33 configurations, reaches a relative error of $10^{-5}$, and its truncated subspaces coincide with the optimal choice; readout errors enlarge the subspace without degrading the accuracy. The tests also show the limitations: the selected subspace coincides with that of inexpensive classical selection, banded matrices require exponentially many Pauli terms in the binary encoding, and Trotter errors populate spurious configurations unless the circuits are deep. An MIT-licensed Python package and notebooks that reproduce the numerical results are provided.

78. 一种用于周期性两相热传导且条件数与网格无关的预条件量子线性求解器#

A Preconditioned Quantum Linear Solver for Periodic Two-Phase Heat Conduction with Mesh-Independent Condition Number

作者: Krishnan Suresh

类别: math.NA, cs.CE, math.AP

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41690v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41690v1

摘要: We consider here computational homogenization of a periodic two-phase conduction problem. Quantum linear-system algorithms for its finite element discretization are limited by the condition number of the stiffness matrix. This condition number grows quadratically with the number of elements per side. Classically, preconditioning by the homogeneous (single-phase) operator bounds the condition number by the conductivity contrast, for every mesh size. On a quantum computer, however, a preconditioner encoded as a separate factor multiplies the subnormalizations, and the quadratic dependency returns. In this paper, we absorb the closed-form inverse square root of the homogeneous operator into the finite element factors. The preconditioned operator then becomes the product of a norm-preserving gradient operator $\mat{V}$, a diagonal matrix of scaled conductivities, and $\mat{V}^T$. Each factor has norm at most one. The block encoding therefore has subnormalization one, and its condition number is bounded by just the conductivity contrast, for every mesh size and in every dimension. The operator $\mat{V}$ is applied as a quantum Fourier transform, a small rotation at each wavenumber, and an inverse transform. Quantum singular value transformation then block-encodes the inverse of the preconditioned operator. We test the method numerically on three periodic cells with up to $10^6$ unknowns. The results confirm that the condition number does not depend on the mesh, and the energy-norm error of the inverse is below the polynomial tolerance at every mesh. On meshes of $4 \times 4$ and $8 \times 8$ elements, we also build the entire inverse as one Qiskit circuit and simulate it by state vector, with the rotation at each wavenumber built from uniformly controlled rotations. The output state agrees with the finite element solution to within the polynomial tolerance.

79. 一种用于计算极端特征对的高斯过程回归增强双扫描参数化幂法#

A Gaussian Process Regression-Enhanced Two-Sweep Parameterized Power Method for Computing Extreme Eigenpairs

作者: Tiantian Sun, Xiaofei Peng, Juan Zhang, Seakweng Vong

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41627v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41627v1

摘要: For computing the dominant eigenvalue and the corresponding eigenvector of a real and symmetric matrix, inspired by the parameterized power method and multistep iteration, we propose a two-sweep parameterized power method, and establish its convergence theory. To further improve the convergence speed of the new method, we use Gaussian process regression (GPR) to predict the quasi-optimal parameter and give a version of the proposed method based on GPR. Numerical experiments show that the two-sweep parameterized power method with quasi-optimal parameters outperforms some existing methods in terms of computing efficiency.

80. 矢量Play模型中单调性的丧失及其对Newton型求解器的影响#

Loss of Monotonicity in the Vector Play Model and Consequences for Newton-Type Solvers

作者: Dominik Cibicki, Klaus Roppert, Manfred Kaltenbacher

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2611.00168v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00168v1

摘要: The vector play model (VPM) replaces the implicit minimization of the energy-based hysteresis model (EBM) by an explicit update. Its tangent contains the product of the Hessians of two unrelated potentials and is unsymmetric. Hence, the VPM has no co-energy density at frozen memory, and the skew part makes $\boldsymbol{H}\mapsto\boldsymbol{B}$ lose monotonicity in a band just outside the pinning circle, into which small time steps drive the sliding states. In composite models, the determinant of the tangent can change sign, which explains published failures of the local inversion $\boldsymbol{B}\mapsto\boldsymbol{H}$, and where it is negative, $\boldsymbol{H}\mapsto\boldsymbol{B}$ is not injective. In the scalar potential formulation, the exact tangent keeps a descent guarantee, whereas symmetric surrogates of the tangent, such as the symmetrized tangent or Broyden-Fletcher-Goldfarb-Shanno (BFGS) updates, lose it and become ascent directions where the skew part dominates, as computations of TEAM Problem 32 confirm. It is therefore recommended to use solvers with an unsymmetric tangent.

81. 关于随机 Cucker-Smale 群集动力学的约化 SPDE 模型#

On reduced SPDE models for stochastic Cucker-Smale flocking dynamics

作者: Kamran Arora, Federico Cornalba, Ana Djurdjevac

类别: math.AP, math.NA, math.PR

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41595v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41595v1

摘要: Flocking refers to a phenomenon in collective motion where particles align their velocities with each other, commonly modelled using Cucker-Smale dynamics. In this work, we introduce and analyse two general reduced SPDE models for stochastic Cucker-Smale particle dynamics that encompass environmental and i.i.d.-type noises. Our models are computationally cheaper alternatives to the underlying particle system and preserve the flocking behaviour. To derive our reduced models, we consider suitable empirical particle densities which depend on position and time, but feature velocity dependency in a multiplicative fashion: this setup crucially allows for a very intuitive and convenient notion of flocking at the SPDE level. In $d=1$ we prove flocking of the reduced SPDE models, and, for the environmental noise, we also quantify the discrepancy between the reduced SPDE and mollified particle system. We also discuss the technical modifications required to extend the results to higher dimensions. We then provide extensive numerical experiments to verify our theoretical results in $d=1$ and to explore the behaviour of our models in $d=2$.

82. 多面体区域上拟一致设计的精确构造#

Exact construction of quasi-uniform designs over polytopal domains

作者: Songlin Yang, Takashi Goda

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41592v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41592v1

摘要: We develop two sampling algorithms for constructing extensible designs with mesh ratio at most two on finite unions of full-dimensional rational convex polytopes. Starting from a rational initial point, both algorithms produce rational coordinates and perform the geometric comparisons governing point selection exactly. This ensures that distance ties and geometric degeneracies are handled without rounding ambiguity, preserving the prescribed selection rules and mesh-ratio guarantee at every stage. The first algorithm, Local Exact Farthest-Point Sampling (LEFPS), implements a prescribed farthest-point rule by local updates of the restricted Voronoi diagram. The second algorithm, Arithmetic-Aware Admissible Sampling (AAS), minimizes the representation length of reduced rational coordinates over admissible fragment vertices and edge midpoints. Since the cost of exact arithmetic depends on coordinate lengths, we analyze both operation counts and bit complexity. For fixed dimension and input, each sampling rule admits an $O(N\log N)$ unit-cost implementation and a separate implementation with $\widetilde O(L_N)$ bit complexity, where $L_N$ is the total length of the output coordinates. On rational interval covers, every admissible sequence from the vertex-midpoint candidate set has logarithmic coordinate height. We also identify the ordered sequences explicitly for LEFPS on interval unions and four-dimensional coordinate-order domains, and for AAS on a rectangle and a nonconvex cross. These descriptions yield direct generation with $O(N)$ unit-cost work and optimal $Θ(N\log N)$ bit work, including exact quality measures at every prefix. Numerical experiments compare local Voronoi maintenance, coordinate growth, and direct generation.

83. 一种高效的随机化分层Tucker分解#

An Efficient Randomized Hierarchical Tucker Decomposition

作者: Bram Leys, Karl Meerbergen, Nick Vannieuwenhoven, Mathijs Vivet

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41554v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41554v1

摘要: We introduce randomized algorithms for computing the hierarchical Tucker decomposition of high-order tensors. Unlike traditional algorithms performed in a sequential leaves-to-root direction, our algorithms traverse the dimension tree in a root-to-leaves direction, leveraging randomized sequential projections to accelerate the computation of intermediate subspaces. This strategy can reduce the computation time by an order of magnitude relative to prior randomized algorithms using the leaves-to-root direction, while guaranteeing quasi-optimal approximation. Although the theoretical error bound is slightly larger than for leaves-to-root methods, in all our experiments the practical error adheres to the classic bound. Our results highlight the method’s potential for scalable tensor decompositions in high-dimensional data analysis.

84. 次扩散驱动的非线性随机微分方程的数值方法#

Numerical methods for nonlinear stochastic differential equations driven by subdiffusion

作者: Qian Guo, Wei Liu, Ruchun Zuo

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41550v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41550v1

摘要: Stochastic differential equations (SDEs) driven by subdiffusion see great applications in anomalous transport and asset price dynamics. Trusted numerical methods are essential for their application in practice. In this paper, for SDEs driven by subdiffusion with superlinear coefficients, we propose a brand-new explicit numerical method. The sharp convergence rate is proved with new techniques. The potential application of the proposed method in numerically solving a generalized Fokker-Planck equation is also illustrated. Numerical simulations are provided to demonstrate the theoretical results.

85. 基于分段圆与尖点的保特征界面重构#

Feature-preserving interface reconstruction using piecewise circles and cusps

作者: Jerry Liu, Kenneth Weiss, Jin Yao

类别: physics.comp-ph, math.NA, physics.flu-dyn

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41305v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41305v1

摘要: We present a geometric interface reconstruction method for Volume-of-Fluid (VOF) simulations that captures both curved interfaces and sharp features using only volume fraction data. We represent interfaces with linear facets, circular arcs, and corner facets formed by two lines or arcs joined within a single cell. We first propagate local orientation constraints through a graph and merge cells to resolve ambiguities, treating topological consistency as a first-class constraint. An optional conservative refinement improves $C^0$ continuity while preserving local volume constraints. Users can independently enable circular facets, corner facets, cell merging, and continuity refinement to balance accuracy and computational cost. In its simplest configuration, our method provides a fast local line-fitting scheme competitive with state-of-the-art PLIC methods. On standard static benchmarks, the full configuration reconstructs identifiable lines, circles, and sufficiently resolved corners to numerical precision on coarse, perturbed Cartesian meshes. On varying-curvature interfaces, it achieves third-order convergence in facet-gap error and approximately first-order convergence in curvature error. These results demonstrate global volume conservation and accurate reconstruction of curved and corner features.

86. 线性逼近类的一般频率原则#

General Frequency Principle for Linear Approximation Classes

作者: Yujun Wang, Yiqi Gu

类别: cs.LG, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-30

arXiv ID: 2610.41179v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41179v1

摘要: In this paper, we study a general frequency principle under gradient descent training for the finite linear approximation classes. Under suitable assumptions, we prove that the learner amplitude relative to the target amplitude vanishes at high frequencies, uniformly throughout training. We also establish sufficient conditions for low-frequency priority, covering single-frequency targets trained separately and two frequency components trained in the same run. This general frequency principle does not imply that lower frequencies always converge faster. Instead, for two frequencies that satisfy certain comparison conditions, one component will take longer to learn than the other; and in common cases, the longer component has higher frequency. Numerical experiments with Legendre and trigonometric bases validate the theoretical results.

87. 用于时间依赖偏微分方程模型降阶的无网格潜动力学模型#

Meshless Latent Dynamics Models for Model Order Reduction of time-dependent Partial Differential Equations

作者: Lorenzo Tomada, Federico Pichi, Gianluigi Rozza

类别: math.NA, cs.LG

发布日期: 2026-10-29

arXiv ID: 2610.41043v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41043v1

摘要: Latent dynamics learning has emerged as a prominent approach for Model Order Reduction (MOR) of time-dependent, parameterized Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). Traditional autoencoder-based approaches are intrinsically tied to fixed spatial discretizations during both training and inference, motivating the development of meshless methodologies. In this context, conditioning provides a natural mechanism for representing temporal evolution through spatially continuous functions, enabling mesh-agnostic Latent Dynamics Models (LDMs). In this work, we introduce a unified mathematical framework for mesh-agnostic LDMs based on conditioning in Neural Fields (NeFs). Leveraging a taxonomy of conditioning mechanisms (weak, strong, and pure), we formulate meshless LDMs as NeFs conditioned on a latent state. We establish a Universal Approximation Theorem (UAT) for this class of models and analyze their stability by deriving Lipschitz bounds for different conditioning mechanisms. Our analysis reveals an expressivity–stability trade-off, providing insight into why more expressive conditioning, despite its higher representational capacity, can potentially degrade performance during temporal extrapolation. Finally, we provide a comprehensive comparison of meshless and mesh-based architectures in the continuous limit of the spatial discretization, showing that meshless models guarantee grid-independent Lipschitz bounds, unlike mesh-based counterparts. Our experiments across Burgers’ equation, Navier–Stokes flow past a cylinder, and linear advection show that meshless LDMs can achieve predictive accuracy comparable to or better than their mesh-based counterparts while using only 2% of the trainable parameters and exhibiting nearly constant error across spatial discretizations.

88. 注意力机制上下文压缩的基于核插值方法#

Kernel-based Interpolatory Methods for Context Compression of Attention Mechanisms

作者: Michel Fabrice Serret, Laura Grigori

类别: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL, math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-29

arXiv ID: 2610.40943v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40943v1

摘要: The bottleneck of attention mechanisms, which are the fundamental building block of modern language models, is their quadratic complexity in terms of context size. There has thus been an increasing interest in the compression of the context, also called KV-cache compression. In this article, we consider the problem of compressing the context of attention mechanisms by first formulating it in the Hilbert space associated with its kernel. In this formalism, attention can be represented as the ratio of two linear maps whose interpolant is also the ratio of interpolants, this allows us to consider the interpolation of the linear maps directly. We thus introduce two novel KV-cache compression methods which we call GAIM and Q-DAIM, based on the Generalized Empirical Interpolation Method (GEIM) and the Q-Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (Q-DEIM), respectively. As the cost of these methods is quadratic in terms of context length, we propose surrogate models based on the Taylor series, the Performer and the Random Fourier Features method which allow the compression to be done in linear time. Finally, we extend this result to the streaming attention setting, providing new methods which allow one to penalize the selection of interpolation nodes based on the previously interpolated context and to update the previous interpolation based on new information. We then provide numerical results on the convergence of the residuals and the output error in future queries and preliminary results on the LongBench-E benchmark showing performance comparable to the state-of-the-art methods. These experiments suggest that the range revealed by our Q-DAIM method could help identify where information is concentrated in the KV cache, thus enabling an efficient compression.

89. 输运方程的节点保界有限元方法#

The nodally bound-preserving finite element method for the transport equation

作者: Nicolás Espinoza-Contreras, Gabriel Barrenechea, Douglas Pacheco

类别: math.NA

发布日期: 2026-10-29

arXiv ID: 2610.40841v1

链接: http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40841v1

摘要: Numerical approximations of transport problems, even when stabilised, often violate the bounds respected by the exact solution. Depending on the problem being solved, this can produce non-physical results, such as negative densities or higher-than-unity concentrations. Various bound-preserving numerical methods exist, yet they are usually either conditionally stable or only first-order accurate. In this context, the present work extends the nodally bound-preserving finite element method to the linear transport equation. As a baseline discretisation we use the least-squares finite element method, which is instrumental in achieving unconditional stability for second-order time stepping. It also leads to symmetric algebraic systems, thereby enabling simple, efficient solvers, which we further enhance through Anderson acceleration. Unconditional stability is proved for the backward Euler and Crank–Nicolson discretisations. We also derive error estimates for the fully discrete second-order scheme and provide various numerical examples, including two-phase incompressible flows.
